<script>
  var xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
  xhr.open('GET', '/designs/74165272/canvas/1/product_images', true);
  xhr.onload = function() {
    if (xhr.status >= 200 && xhr.status < 400) {
      var data = JSON.parse(xhr.responseText);
      var productImages = data.images;
      window.TeePublic.ProductImages = productImages;
 
      // Update the gallery if a product selection has been made
      const productImageSwapQueue = window.TeePublic && window.TeePublic.ProductImageSwapQueue;
      if (!productImageSwapQueue) return;
 
      window.TeePublic.ProductHelper.updateGallery(
        productImageSwapQueue.gallery,
        productImageSwapQueue.productId
      );
 
    } else {
      console.error('Error: ' + xhr.status);
    }
  };
 
  xhr.onerror = function(error) {
    console.error('Error: ' + error);
  };
 
  xhr.send();
 
  function checkProductImageSwapQueue() {
    
  }
</script>

    <section class="about" id="about">
      <div class="container about-grid">
        <div class="about-copy reveal">
          <span class="eyebrow" data-i18n="about.eyebrow">About the group</span>
          <h2 data-i18n="about.h2">Built quietly, since 1980 &mdash; trusted across the region.</h2>
          <p data-i18n="about.p1">
            Founded in Damascus, Al Bunni Group is a privately-held holding of engineering companies. Each member operates under its own brand and discipline, yet draws on a shared culture of careful design, durable build quality, and long-term client relationships.
          </p>
          <blockquote class="pullquote" data-i18n="about.quote">
            Quietly engineered. Loudly trusted.
          </blockquote>
          <p data-i18n="about.p2">
            We work across government, aviation, healthcare, education, energy, finance, hospitality and culture &mdash; from large commandcenters and airport systems to integrated workplaces and power continuity.
          </p>
        </div>

      function applyLang(lang) {
        if (lang !== 'en' && lang !== 'ar') lang = 'en';
        var t = I18N[lang];
        document.documentElement.lang = lang;
        document.documentElement.dir = lang === 'ar' ? 'rtl' : 'ltr';
        document.title = t.metaTitle;
        var meta = document.getElementById('metaDesc');
        if (meta) meta.setAttribute('content', t.metaDescription);

        document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n]').forEach(function (el) {
          var k = el.getAttribute('data-i18n');
          if (t[k] !== undefined) el.textContent = t[k];
        });
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n-aria-label]').forEach(function (el) {
          var k = el.getAttribute('data-i18n-aria-label');
          if (t[k] !== undefined) el.setAttribute('aria-label', t[k]);
        });
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n-placeholder]').forEach(function (el) {
          var k = el.getAttribute('data-i18n-placeholder');
          if (t[k] !== undefined) el.setAttribute('placeholder', t[k]);
        });
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n-alt]').forEach(function (el) {
          var k = el.getAttribute('data-i18n-alt');
          if (t[k] !== undefined) el.setAttribute('alt', t[k]);
        });

        var btnEn = document.getElementById('langEn');
        var btnAr = document.getElementById('langAr');
        if (btnEn) btnEn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', lang === 'en' ? 'true' : 'false');
        if (btnAr) btnAr.setAttribute('aria-pressed', lang === 'ar' ? 'true' : 'false');

        try { localStorage.setItem('abg-lang', lang); } catch (e) {}
      }

      var stored = null;
      try { stored = localStorage.getItem('abg-lang'); } catch (e) {}
      var initialLang = stored === 'ar' || stored === 'en' ? stored : 'en';applyLang(initialLang);

      var btnEn = document.getElementById('langEn');
      var btnAr = document.getElementById('langAr');
      if (btnEn) btnEn.addEventListener('click', function () { applyLang('en'); });
      if (btnAr) btnAr.addEventListener('click', function () { applyLang('ar'); });

      var nav = document.getElementById('nav');
      var toggle = document.getElementById('navToggle');
      var links = document.getElementById('navLinks');
      var hero = document.querySelector('.hero');

      var setScrolled = function () {
        var y = window.scrollY || window.pageYOffset || 0;
        if (y > 16) nav.classList.add('scrolled');
        else nav.classList.remove('scrolled');

        var docH = document.documentElement.scrollHeight - window.innerHeight;
        var pct = docH > 0 ? Math.min(100, (y / docH) * 100) : 0;
        nav.style.setProperty('--scroll-progress', pct.toFixed(2) + '%');

        if (hero) {
          if (y > 80) hero.classList.add('cue-fade');
          else hero.classList.remove('cue-fade');
        }
      };
      setScrolled();
      window.addEventListener('scroll', setScrolled, { passive: true });
      window.addEventListener('resize', setScrolled, { passive: true });

      toggle.addEventListener('click', function () {
        var open = links.classList.toggle('open');
        toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', open ? 'true' : 'false');
      });

      links.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
        var t = e.target;
        if (t && t.tagName === 'A' && links.classList.contains('open')) {
          links.classList.remove('open');
          toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
        }
      });

      document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
        if (!links.classList.contains('open')) return;
        if (e.target === toggle || toggle.contains(e.target)) return;
        if (!links.contains(e.target)) {
          links.classList.remove('open');
          toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
        }
      });

      var y = document.getElementById('year');
      if (y) y.textContent = new Date().getFullYear();

      var reduce = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
      var hasIO = 'IntersectionObserver' in window;
      var targets = document.querySelectorAll('.reveal, .reveal-stagger');

      if (reduce || !hasIO) {
        targets.forEach(function (el) { el.classList.add('in'); });
      } else {
        var io = new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
          entries.forEach(function (entry) {
            if (entry.isIntersecting) {
              entry.target.classList.add('in');
              io.unobserve(entry.target);
            }});
        }, { threshold: 0.15, rootMargin: '0px 0px -40px 0px' });
        targets.forEach(function (el) { io.observe(el); });
      }

      // Count-up animation for stats with [data-count]
      var countEls = document.querySelectorAll('[data-count]');
      var runCount = function (el) {
        var end = parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-count')) || 0;
        if (reduce) { el.textContent = end; return; }
        var dur = Math.min(1800, 700 + end * 22);
        var start = performance.now();
        var step = function (now) {
          var t = Math.min(1, (now - start) / dur);
          var eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 3);
          var v = Math.round(end * eased);
          el.textContent = v;
          if (t < 1) requestAnimationFrame(step);
          else el.textContent = end;
        };
        requestAnimationFrame(step);
      };
      if (hasIO) {
        var countIo = new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
          entries.forEach(function (entry) {
            if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
            countIo.unobserve(entry.target);
            runCount(entry.target);
          });
        }, { threshold: 0.4 });
        countEls.forEach(function (el) { countIo.observe(el); });
      } else {
        countEls.forEach(runCount);
      }

      // Marquee — duplicate the row content for seamless infinite scroll
      var marqueeRow = document.getElementById('marqueeRow');
      if (marqueeRow && !reduce) {
        marqueeRow.innerHTML = marqueeRow.innerHTML + marqueeRow.innerHTML;}

      // Magnetic buttons (pointer-only)
      var canHover = window.matchMedia('(hover: hover) and (pointer: fine)').matches;
      if (canHover && !reduce) {
        document.querySelectorAll('.magnetic').forEach(function (btn) {
          var raf = null;
          btn.addEventListener('mousemove', function (e) {
            var r = btn.getBoundingClientRect();
            var cx = r.left + r.width / 2;
            var cy = r.top + r.height / 2;
            var dx = (e.clientX - cx) * 0.18;
            var dy = (e.clientY - cy) * 0.18;
            if (raf) cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
            raf = requestAnimationFrame(function () {
              btn.style.transform = 'translate(' + dx.toFixed(2) + 'px, ' + dy.toFixed(2) + 'px)';
            });
          });
          btn.addEventListener('mouseleave', function () {
            if (raf) cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
            btn.style.transform = '';
          });
        });
      }

      // Subtle 3D tilt on sector tiles (pointer-only, capped)
      if (canHover && !reduce) {
        document.querySelectorAll('.sector').forEach(function (tile) {
          varraf = null;
          tile.addEventListener('mousemove', function (e) {
            var r = tile.getBoundingClientRect();
            var px = ((e.clientX - r.left) / r.width) - 0.5;
            var py = ((e.clientY - r.top) / r.height) - 0.5;
            var rx = (-py * 4).toFixed(2);
            var ry = (px * 4).toFixed(2);
            if (raf) cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
            raf = requestAnimationFrame(function () {
              tile.style.transform = 'translateY(-6px) perspective(900px) rotateX(' + rx + 'deg) rotateY(' + ry + 'deg)';
            });
          });
          tile.addEventListener('mouseleave', function () {
            if (raf) cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
            tile.style.transform = '';
          });
        });
      }

      // Floating-label has-value for selects (input/textarea handled via :not(:placeholder-shown))
      var selects = document.querySelectorAll('.field select');
      var syncSelect = function (sel) {
        var field = sel.closest('.field');
        if (!field) return;
        if (sel.value && sel.value.length) field.classList.add('has-value');
        else field.classList.remove('has-value');
      };
      selects.forEach(function (sel) {
        syncSelect(sel);
        sel.addEventListener('change', function () { syncSelect(sel); });
      });
    })();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
